In financial year 2023/2024 (FY2023/2024), the Group’s profit to        於二零二三╱二零二四財政年度，由於我們專
equity shareholders grew substantially, by 155%, as a result of our     注改善執行中國內地的銷售策略並將產品創新
focus on improving field sales execution in Mainland China and          應用於香港業務，本集團股權持有人應佔溢利
leveraging portfolio innovation in the Hong Kong Operation. This        大幅增長 155%。然而，部分增長被我們目前正
was partly offset by manufacturing attainment issues in Australia       在處理的澳洲生產達標問題抵銷。
that we are now addressing.

Group revenue in FY2023/2024 rose 1% over the previous year,            於二零二三╱二零二四財政年度，撇除匯率影
excluding currency impact. The drop of 3% in the first half of the      響後，本集團收入較上一年度增長 1%。儘管上
financial year was more than offset in the second half by Mainland      半年財政年度收入下跌 3%，但中國內地收入增
China’s 10% revenue growth in local currency terms and solid            長 10%（以當地貨幣計算）且香港業務表現穩健，
performance by the Hong Kong Operation.                                 下半年的表現足以彌補上半年的下跌。

Following improvement in the Group’s financial performance, and         隨著本集團財務表現及現金狀況有所改善，董
in its cash position, the Board of Directors recommended a final        事會建議派發二零二三╱二零二四財政年度的
dividend of HK6.3 cents per ordinary share for FY2023/2024,             末 期 股 息 每 股 普 通 股 6.3 港 仙，惟 須 待 股 東 於
subject to the shareholders’ approval at the Annual General Meeting     二零二四年八月十九日舉行的股東週年大會上
to be held on 19th August 2024. This, along with the interim            批准，方可作實。連同中期股息每股普通股 1.4
dividend of HK1.4 cents per ordinary share, brings the total dividend   港仙，二零二三╱二零二四財政年度的股息總
for FY2023/2024 to HK7.7 cents per ordinary share.                      額達至每股普通股 7.7 港仙。


Encouraging growth in Mainland China and Hong                           中國內地及香港業務增長令人鼓舞
Kong Operation
Mainland China attained substantial growth in operating profit,         在嚴格執行銷售策略及組織能力提升所帶動下，
driven by disciplined sales execution and improved organisational       中國內地經營溢利錄得大幅增長。
capabilities.

compared with last year, primarily due to successful product            由於產品創新－我們核心業務優勢之一取得成
innovation – one of our core business strengths – and the return to     功，以及維他天地的學校業務恢復正常營運。
normal trading of the Vitaland school business.

    costs in a tight supply environment. These issues are currently being     這些問題。
    addressed with a high sense of urgency.

    as growing export tofu sales were offset by soft local demand             因豆腐出口銷售的增長被當地需求疲軟、以及
    and slower beverage sales in preparation for our switch to a new          為準備轉換至新飲品分銷商而導致飲品銷售放
    beverages distributor. Our Philippines joint-venture business grew        緩所抵銷。我們菲律賓合營公司業務的家庭裝
    strongly in both the multi-serve and on-the-go platforms.                 產品及便利店平台業務均錄得強勁增長。

10   Vitasoy International Holdings Ltd. 維他奶國際集團有限公司
          Group Chief Executive Officer’s Report/Business Review
          集 團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧




Group revenue grew by 1% net of currency impact in FY2023/2024          撇除匯率影響後，在下半年穩健增長 6% 的帶動
after recovering from a 3% drop during the first half of the year,      下，本集團收入由上半年下跌 3% 中恢復過來，
driven by a solid 6% growth in the second half.                         二零二三╱二零二四財政年度，整體增長 1%。

This result is primarily attributable to the accelerating performance   本年度的業績主要歸因於中國業務增長加速。
of our China business. In Mainland China, revenue grew 10%              以當地貨幣計算，中國內地的收入於下半年增
in local currency terms during the second half of the year, with        長 10%，維他奶及維他品牌均因有效改善執行
both VITASOY and VITA brands returning to growth as a result            銷售策略而恢復增長。香港業務透過強勁的核
of improved sales execution. The Hong Kong Operation achieved           心組合表現及選擇性產品創新實現了個位數增
robust single digit growth through strong core portfolio performance    長。於澳洲，生產達標問題暫時窒礙增長及盈

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE
       REPORT
    企業管治報告

                              Annual Report 2023/2024 年度報告   25
               Corporate Governance Report
               企 業管治報告




     Vitasoy International Holdings Limited (the “Company”) is firmly         維他奶國際集團有限公司（「本公司」）堅守高水
     committed to a high level of corporate governance and adherence to       平的企業管治，並時刻遵守注重具透明度、獨
     the governance principles and practices emphasising transparency,        立性、問責、負責任與公平之管治原則及常規。
     independence, accountability, responsibility and fairness. These         本公司董事會（「董事會」）或其授權之董事會委
     principles and practices are regularly reviewed, enhanced and            員會定期檢討、改進並更新該等原則及常規，
     updated by the Board of Directors (the “Board”) or its delegated Board   以反映不斷轉變的監管規定及企業管治發展。

affordable nutrition to help improve the lives of others. Since then,   士深受啟發，為大眾提供豐富且價格相宜的營
we have built on his principles to champion sustainable practices       養飲料，以改善大眾生活。自此我們秉承羅博
across our business. We are leading the transition towards more         士的初心，在業務經營中倡導可持續性。公司
sustainable plant-based nutrition. Our founding purpose continues       創辦的理念到今天仍然啟發著我們，繼續熱衷

     Board Composition and Diversity                                         董事會成員組成及多元化
     The Board has adopted a Board Diversity Policy to set out the           董事會於二零一三年六月採納董事會成員多元
     objectives and the factors to be considered for achieving the           化政策，當中載列董事會成員多元化的目的以
     diversity of the Board in June 2013. The board diversity will be        及達致多元化的考慮因素。董事會成員多元化
     achieved through a number of factors, including but not limited to      將透過一系列因素實現，包括但不限於經驗、
     experience, professional qualification, knowledge, gender, ethnicity    專 業 資 格、知 識、性 別、種 族 及 年 齡。董 事 會
     and age. The Board Diversity Policy has been updated in March           成員多元化政策已於二零二二年三月更新，以
     2022 to expressly include the disclosure of targets and timelines for   明確納入披露實現董事會成員性別多元化之目
     achieving gender diversity at Board level. The Board Diversity Policy   標及時間表。董事會成員多元化政策已刊登於
     has been published on the Company’s website. The Remuneration           本公司網站。薪酬及提名委員會將每年檢討該
     and Nomination Committee (the “R&N Committee”) will review this         政策及監察其執行，確保其持續之成效以及符
     policy annually and monitor the implementation to ensure continued      合監管要求及良好企業管治常規。
     effectiveness and compliance with regulatory requirements and good
     corporate governance practices.

     During the year, the Board, through the R&N Committee, had              年內，董事會透過薪酬及提名委員會檢討董事
     reviewed the structure, size, composition and diversity of the Board    會的架構、規模、組成及多元化以及董事會成
     and the Board Diversity Policy, to ensure the Board’s composition       員多元化政策，以確保董事會的組成符合企業
     complies with the CG Code with an appropriate mix of skills,            管治守則，並具備與本公司策略、管治及業務

      Key Features or Mechanisms                       Implementation and Review of Effectiveness
      主要特點或機制                                          執行及檢討成效
      Directors Conflict of Interest Policy            The Company has policy and procedures in place to avoid any
      董事利益衝突政策                                         potential conflict of interests and not to weaken the objectivity
                                                       and integrity of the Board for decisions making. Under the
                                                       Policy, any Director has to declare his/her directorships outside
                                                       the Company, interests in controlling company, public office
                                                       appointment and any other interests that might be considered
                                                       prejudicial to his/her independence and has to declare his/her
                                                       interests in any transaction in which he/she may have interest and
                                                       abstain from voting on such transaction.
                                                       本公司已制定政策及程序以避免任何潛在利益衝突，且不會削弱
                                                       董事會決策的客觀性及誠信性。根據該政策，任何一位董事必須
                                                       申報其於本公司以外的董事職務、於控股公司中的權益、公職任
                                                       命及任何其他可能被認為有礙其獨立性的利益，並須申報其於可
                                                       能擁有利益的任何交易中的權益，並就該交易放棄投票。

      Open Board Culture                               INEDs in family businesses will generally play a more active role in
      開明的董事會文化                                         corporate governance area. Our Board culture under the leadership
                                                       of Executive Chairman, encourages questions and challenges from
                                                       directors in particular from INEDs who may hold different views
                                                       from the Executive Directors and Senior Management and their
                                                       comments and concerns will be closely followed up and addressed
                                                       by the Executive Directors and Senior Management.
                                                       家族企業中的獨立非執行董事一般會於企業管治方面發揮更為
                                                       積極的作用。在執行主席領導下，我們的董事會文化鼓勵董事（尤
                                                       其是獨立非執行董事）提出問題及質疑，彼等可能與執行董事及
                                                       高層管理人員持有不同觀點，而執行董事及高層管理人員將密切
                                                       跟進及應對彼等的意見及關注。

      No equity-based remuneration with                Director fees payable to Director, including INEDs, are fixed fees
      performance-related elements to INEDs            without any discretionary element nor they are remunerated with
      獨立非執行董事未曾授予與表現績效相關                               equity-based awards. Directors Fees are also determined by the
      的股本權益酬金                                          R&N Committee and the Board with reference to the roles and
                                                       responsibilities taken up by the Director and benchmarking against
                                                       peers.
                                                       應付董事（包括獨立非執行董事）的董事袍金為固定數額，並無
                                                       任何酌情因素，而酬金亦無包括以股權為基礎的獎勵。董事袍金
                                                       亦由薪酬及提名委員會與董事會參照董事所承擔的角色及職責
                                                       以及行業標準而釐定。

     Board Proceedings                                                         董事會程序
     The Board met five times in FY2023/2024 to discuss and approve            董事會於二零二三╱二零二四財政年度內召開
     the Group’s long term strategic plan; to review and monitor the           五次會議，包括討論及批准本集團之長期策略
     financial and business performance of the Group and each of               規劃；審視及監察本集團及各營運單位之財務
     its operating units; to consider and approve annual budget, the           及業務表現；考慮及審批年度預算及財務報告；
     financial reports; to review and approve sustainability report and        審閱及批准可持續發展報告及與企業管治及可
     various corporate governance and sustainability related policies          持續發展相關之各種政策及常規；並監察及審
     and practices; and to monitor and review the effectiveness of the         視本集團風險管理表現之成效。
     Group’s risk management performance.

     The Board and its Committees are supplied with full and timely            董事會及其委員會均適時獲得本公司所有相關
     information in relation to the Company, which enables them to             資料，以履行職責。本公司已有既定程序致使
     discharge their responsibilities. There are established procedures        董事在適當的情況下，為履行其職責及責任而
     for Directors to seek independent professional advice for them to         諮詢獨立專業意見，費用由本公司支付。
     discharge their duties and responsibilities, where appropriate, at the
     Company’s expenses.

     Directors have made active participation in the Board meetings,           年內，董事均積極參與董事會會議、董事委員
     Board Committees meetings and the Annual General Meeting                  會 會 議 及 股 東 週 年 大 會。以 下 為 二 零 二 三 ╱
     during the year. Below is the schedule of meetings held during            二零二四財政年度舉行的會議時間表：

Appointment, Re-Election and Removal of                                    董事之委任、重選及罷免
Directors

The appointment of a new Director is made on the recommendation            本公司乃根據本公司薪酬及提名委員會之建議
by the R&N Committee of the Company or by shareholders in a                或通過股東大會由股東委任新董事。股東可根

Company and the Director Nomination Policy. The nomination                 上刊載。凡董事會委任之董事均須於下一屆股
procedures by shareholders have been published on the website              東大會上告退。

     Directors acknowledge their responsibilities for preparing the              董事對編製本公司財務報表承擔責任。董事並
     financial statements of the Company. Directors are not aware of any         不知悉有任何重大不明朗之事件或情況可能會

     significant doubt upon the Company’s ability to continue as a going         二零二四財政年度之全年財務報表乃按持續經

     and policies adopted by the Company have been thoroughly                    後再提交董事會採納。

     the Board.

     Risk Management and Internal Control                                     風險管理及內部監控
     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management        董事會明白其肩負風險管理及內部監控系統之
     and internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness.          責任，並檢討其成效。該系統之設計旨在管理
     The systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate the             而非消除無法達成業務目標之風險，並僅可為
     risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and can only provide     避免發生重大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕
     reasonable and not absolute assurance against material misstatement      對之保證。為實現此目標，董事會確保已制定
     or loss. To achieve this, the Board ensures that there is an effective   有效之風險管治架構及持續風險管理程序，以
     framework of risk governance and ongoing risk management process         達致本公司業務之長久成功。
     to promote the long-term success of the Company.

     a continuing process in the Company. Our internal control system is       本公司之內部監控系統旨在合理保證：
     designed to give reasonable assurance that:

     •     Assets are prudently safeguarded;                                   •   資產獲得審慎保障；

     •     Business activities are conducted in an effective and efficient     •   以有效及高效之方式進行業務活動；
           manner;

     •     Laws and regulations relevant to the business are complied.         •   遵守與業務相關之法律及法規。

     controls while the Board and the Audit Committee oversee the              則監察現有內部監控系統執行之成效。

Risk Governance Structure                                             風險管治架構
The risk governance structure consists of three layers of roles and   風險管治架構包括下列三個層面之角色及職責：
responsibilities as explained below:

 Layer                           Roles and Responsibilities
 層面                              職務及職責
 Oversight                       Audit Committee, acting on behalf of the Board
 監督                              •     Oversees the Company’s risk management and internal control on an ongoing
                                       basis.
                                 •     Oversees those risks that warrant the Committee’s attention and supervise risk
                                       management process as part of good corporate governance.
                                 代表董事會之審核委員會
                                 •     持續監督本公司之風險管理及內部監控。
                                 •     監督委員會需要注意之該等風險並監督風險管理程序，以配合良好企業管治。


                                 Group Internal Audit
                                 •    Conducts independent review on the effectiveness of risk management and
                                      internal control system and provides reasonable assurance to Audit Committee.
                                 集團內部審計
                                 •    對風險管理及內部監控系統之成效進行獨立審閱，並向審核委員會提供合理
                                      保證。

 Risk Monitoring & Review        Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee & Entity CEOs
 風險監察及審閱                         •     Design, implement and monitor the risk management and internal control
                                       system.
                                 •     Provide assurance to the Audit Committee on the effectiveness of risk
                                       management and internal control system.
                                 企業風險管理執行委員會及地方公司行政總裁
                                 •     設計、實施及監察風險管理及內部監控系統。
                                 •     向審核委員會提供有關風險管理及內部監控系統成效之保證。

     Risk Management Process                                                     風險管理程序
     Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,            我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財
     financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business           務 活 動、合 規、策 略 發 展 與 業 務 規 劃、投 資 決
     planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes           定及外界因素，程序如下：
     are as follows:

     •       Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to         •   預 測：所 有 員 工 均 參 與 識 別 不 同 業 務 流
             different business processes and relevant to the Company’s              程及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威
             objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual         脅。為 便 於 進 行 適 當 評 估，我 們 將 風 險
             staff. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including reputation,       分為六個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、
             strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation) to facilitate        流動資金、信貸及營運）。
             the assessment appropriately.

     •       Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of impact      •   評 估：按 照 董 事 會 設 定 之 風 險 偏 好，評
             and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set by the             估 每 項 經 識 別 風 險 的 影 響 及 可 能 性。風
             Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated according           險 之 影 響 及 可 能 性 將 根 據 過 往 記 錄、統
             to the historical records, statistical analyses and relevant            計 分 析、相 關 經 驗 及 管 理 層 之 專 業 判 斷
             experiences, specialist and expert judgments by management.             進行評級。集團風險管理促進評估流程，
             Group Risk Management facilitates the evaluation process                包括協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審
             including the summary discussion in the Enterprise Risk                 核委員會之討論。
             Management Executive Committee and Audit Committee.

•   Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk           •   控 制：各 項 風 險 將 配 對 專 責 管 理 人 員，
    as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management           成 為 風 險 負 責 人，以 有 效 管 理 風 險。風

    defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents, control           量 度 級 別 基 準、所 發 生 之 相 關 事 故、內
    effectiveness and audit results. Any change in current status          部 監 控 程 序 之 成 效 以 及 審 計 結 果。風 險

    the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation plan with           人 將 協 調 減 輕 風 險 計 劃 之 行 動 細 節、負
    action details, responsible person and timeline to address the         責人及時間表，以處理風險情況。
    status.

    requirement was approved by the Board of Directors under               奶 風 險 管 理 之 成 效。集 團 風 險 管 理 定 期

     •     Managing ESG risks with risk management framework                  •   透 過 風 險 管 理 框 架 以 管 理 環 境、社 會 及

     Dividend Policy                                                           股息政策
     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to            董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution of           東向董事會及管理層提供指引。

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following criteria      就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及
     and consideration must be taken:                                          考慮因素：

     –     Sharing of success with shareholders                                －    與股東共享成果

     –     Liquidity and capital requirement                                   －    流動資金及資本要求

     –     Historical trend of dividend payout                                 －    過往股息派付之趨勢

     –     Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                   －    與業內競爭對手之標準比較

     –     Fulfilment of financial covenants                                   －    符合財務契約

     –     Taxation impact                                                     －    稅務影響

     –     Restrictions                                                        －    限制條件

     –     Other factors that may deem relevant                                －    其他視為相關之因素

     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders to align         本公司計劃配合扣除一次性事件影響後之股東

     in addition to interim or final dividends. The determination to pay       不時酌情決定是否派發股息以及所派發股息之

We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Vitasoy          本 核 數 師（以 下 簡 稱「我 們」）已 審 計 列 載 於 第

information.

     Key audit matters                                                         關鍵審計事項

      Revenue recognition from sales to distributors and retailers
      確認來自分銷商和零售商銷售的收入

      Refer to note 3 and the accounting policies in note 1(u) to the consolidated financial statements.
      請參閱綜合財務報表附註 3 及附註 1(u) 的會計政策。

      The Key Audit Matter                                         How the matter was addressed in our audit
      關鍵審計事項                                                       我們的審計如何處理該事項

      The Group’s revenue is principally generated from sales      Our audit procedures to assess the recognition of revenue
      of food and beverages to a large number of distributors      from sales to distributors and retailers included the
      and retailers.                                               following:
      貴集團的收入主要源於向眾多分銷商和零售商銷售食                                      我們就評估有關確認來自分銷商和零售商銷售的收入的
      品及飲品。                                                        審計程序包括以下各項：

      Revenue from sales to distributors and retailers is          •    assessing the design, implementation and operating
      recognised when the customer takes possession of and              effectiveness of management’s key internal controls
      accepts the Group’s food and beverage products, which             over revenue recognition;

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存

auditor’s report that includes our opinion. This report is made solely   我們是按照香港《公司條例》第 405 條的規定，
to you, as a body, in accordance with section 405 of the Hong Kong       僅向整體成員報告。除此以外，我們的報告不
Companies Ordinance, and for no other purpose. We do not assume          可用作其他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，
responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the   對任何其他人士負責或承擔法律責任。
contents of this report.

can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,            引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能
individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected      影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴財務報表所作出
to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of       的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
these consolidated financial statements.

1      Material accounting policies                                        1     重大會計政策

(a)    Statement of compliance                                             (a)   遵例聲明
These financial statements have been prepared in accordance with           本 財 務 報 表 是 按 照 所 有 適 用 之《香 港 財 務 報 告

(b)    Basis of preparation of the financial statements                    (b)   財務報表之編製基準
The consolidated financial statements for the year ended 31st March        截至二零二四年三月三十一日止年度之綜合財
2024 comprise the Company and its subsidiaries (together referred          務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）
to as the “Group”) and the Group’s interest in a joint venture.            以及本集團於一間合營公司之權益。

statements is the historical cost basis except that derivative financial   按公允值計算外，編製財務報表是以歷史成本
instruments are stated at fair value as explained in the accounting        作為計量基準。
policies set out in note 1(t).

amount and fair value less costs to sell (see note 1(y)).                  成本兩者中之較低者列賬（見附註 1(y)）。

The preparation of financial statements in conformity with                 管理層須在編製符合《香港財務報告準則》之財
HKFRSs requires management to make judgements, estimates and               務報表時作出會影響會計政策應用，以及資產、
assumptions that affect the application of policies and reported           負債、收入及支出之報告金額之判斷、估計及
amounts of assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The estimates         假設。此等估計及相關假設是根據以往經驗和
and associated assumptions are based on historical experience and          管理層因應當時情況認為合理之多項其他因素
various other factors that are believed to be reasonable under the         作出，其結果構成當管理層在無法依循其他途
circumstances, the results of which form the basis of making the           徑即時得知資產與負債之賬面值時所作出判斷
judgements about carrying values of assets and liabilities that are not    之基礎。實際結果可能有別於估計數額。
readily apparent from other sources. Actual results may differ from
these estimates.

The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an                管理層會不斷審閱各項估計和相關假設。倘若
ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in         會計估計之修訂只影響某一期間，則該修訂便
the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only   會在估計修訂期間內確認，或如果修訂對當期
that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods if the    和未來期間均有影響，則在作出修訂之期間和
revision affects both current and future periods.                          未來期間確認。

Gains or losses arising from the retirement or disposal of an item           報廢或出售物業、廠房及設備項目所產生之盈
of property, plant and equipment are determined as the difference            虧為出售該項目所得款項淨額與該項目賬面值
between the net disposal proceeds and the carrying amount of the             之差額，並於報廢或出售當日在損益內確認。
item and are recognised in profit or loss on the date of retirement or
disposal.

Depreciation is calculated to write off the cost of items of property,       折舊乃按物業、廠房及設備項目之成本減去其
plant and equipment, less their estimated residual value, if any, using      估計剩餘價值（如有），在其估計可使用年期採
the straight line method over their estimated useful lives as follows:       用直線法以下列方式撇銷計算：

–      Freehold land is not depreciated.                                     －     永久業權之土地並無折舊。

–      Buildings situated on freehold land are depreciated over their        －     於永久業權之土地上之樓宇以不超過 25
       estimated useful lives, being no more than 25 years.                        年之估計可使用年期折舊。

–      Leasehold land and buildings are depreciated over the shorter         －     租賃土地及樓宇按未屆滿租賃期及估計
       of the unexpired term of lease and their estimated useful lives,            可使用年期（不超過 50 年）兩者中之較短
       being no more than 50 years.                                                者折舊。

–      Other plant and equipment:                                            －     其他廠房及設備：


       Factory machinery and equipment                        4 – 25 years         工廠機器及設備                  4 – 25 年
       Fixtures, furniture and office equipment               3 – 12 years         裝置、傢俬及辦公室設備              3 – 12 年
       Motor vehicles                                         4 – 10 years         汽車                       4 – 10 年

No provision for depreciation is made for construction in progress           直至有關資產已大致完成及可供使用前，並無
until such time when the assets are substantially completed and              就在建工程作出折舊撥備。
ready for use.

Where parts of an item of property, plant and equipment have                 倘一項物業、廠房及設備中之各部份有不同之
different useful lives, the cost of the item is allocated on a reasonable    可使用年期，該項目之成本將合理地分配至各
basis between the parts and each part is depreciated separately.             部份，而各部份則獨立計提折舊。

Both the useful life of an asset and its residual value, if any, are         資產之可使用年期及其剩餘價值（如有）將於每
reviewed annually.                                                           年檢討。

(j)    Credit loss and impairment of assets                                    (j)    信貸虧損及資產減值

The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses               本 集 團 為 按 攤 銷 成 本 計 量 之 金 融 資 產（包 括 現
(“ECLs”) on financial assets measured at amortised cost (including             金及現金等值項目以及應收賬款及其他應收款）
cash and cash equivalents, and trade and other receivables).                   確認預期信貸虧損之虧損撥備。

Measurement of ECLs                                                            預期信貸虧損之計量
ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses. Credit              預期信貸虧損乃信貸虧損之概率加權估計。信
losses are measured as the present value of all expected cash                  貸 虧 損 以 所 有 預 期 現 金 差 額（即 根 據 合 約 應 付
shortfalls (i.e. the difference between the cash flows due to the              予本集團之現金流量與本集團預期收取之現金
Group in accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the              流量之間的差額）的現值計量。
Group expects to receive).

Where the effect of discounting is material, the expected cash                 倘折現之影響屬重大，則預期現金差額將採用
shortfalls are discounted using the effective interest rate determined         應收賬款及其他應收款初始確認時釐定之實際
at initial recognition or an approximation thereof for trade and other         利率或其近似值折現。
receivables.

The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs is the                      估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮之最長期間為本集
maximum contractual period over which the Group is exposed to                  團面臨信貸風險之最長合約期間。
credit risk.

            all possible default events over the expected lives of the items            採用預期信貸虧損模式之項目在預期可

                                                                                        導致之虧損。

     a provision matrix based on the Group’s historical credit loss               預期信貸虧損乃使用基於本集團過往信貸虧損
     experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors and        經驗之撥備矩陣進行估算，並就於報告日期債
     an assessment of both the current and forecast general economic              務人之特定因素以及對當前及預測整體經濟狀
     conditions at the reporting date.                                            況之評估予以調整。

     Write-off policy                                                              撇銷政策
     The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written off (either         倘金融資產並無實際收回前景，則其賬面總值
     partially or in full) to the extent that there is no realistic prospect of   （部分或全部）將會被撇銷。該情況通常出現在
     recovery. This is generally the case when the Group determines that           本集團認為債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生
     the debtor does not have assets or sources of income that could               足夠現金流量以償還將予撇銷之金額。
     generate sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject to the
     write-off.

–      Calculation of recoverable amount                                    －      計算可收回金額

       The recoverable amount of an asset is the greater of its fair               資產之可收回金額為其公允值減去出售
       value less costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing value           成 本 及 使 用 價 值 兩 者 中 之 較 高 者。於 評
       in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to                   估 使 用 價 值 時，會 使 用 除 稅 前 折 現 率 將
       their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects             估 計 未 來 現 金 流 量 折 現 至 其 現 值。該 折
       current market assessments of time value of money and the                   現率反映市場當時所評估之貨幣時間價
       risks specific to the asset. Where an asset does not generate               值 和 該 資 產 之 獨 有 風 險。倘 個 別 資 產 所
       cash inflows largely independent of those from other assets,                產生之現金流入基本上不能獨立於其他
       the recoverable amount is determined for the smallest group                 資 產 所 產 生 之 現 金 流 入，則 就 獨 立 產 生
       of assets that generates cash inflows independently (i.e. a                 現金流入之最小資產組合（即現金產生單
       cash-generating unit).                                                      位）釐定可收回金額。

       An impairment loss is recognised in profit or loss if the carrying          資產或其所屬之現金產生單位之賬面值
       amount of an asset, or the cash-generating unit to which it                 高 於 其 可 收 回 金 額 時，則 會 於 損 益 確 認
       belongs, exceeds its recoverable amount. Impairment losses                  減 值 虧 損。就 現 金 產 生 單 位 確 認 之 減 值
       recognised in respect of cash-generating units are allocated                虧損會首先分配予削減已分配至該現金
       first to reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill allocated               產生單位（或一組單位）之任何商譽之賬
       to the cash-generating unit (or group of units) and then, to                面值，然後按比例削減該單位（或一組單
       reduce the carrying amount of the other assets in the unit (or              位）內其他資產之賬面值，惟資產賬面值
       group of units) on a pro rata basis, except that the carrying               不可下調至低於其個別公允值減去出售
       value of an asset will not be reduced below its individual fair             成本（如能計量）或使用價值（如能釐定）。
       value less costs of disposal (if measurable), or value in use (if
       determinable).

     (k)     Inventories                                                         (k)     存貨
     Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable value.      存貨按成本值及可變現淨值兩者中之較低者入賬。

     Cost is calculated using the weighted average method and comprises          成本值乃以加權平均方法計算，並包括所有購
     all costs of purchase, costs of conversion and other costs incurred in      貨成本、加工成本及將存貨運往其現時地點及
     bringing the inventories to their present location and condition.           達至現有狀態之其他成本。

     Net realisable value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary         可變現淨值指正常業務過程中之估計售價減去
     course of business less the estimated costs of completion and the           完成交易之估計成本及進行銷售所需之估計成本。
     estimated costs necessary to make the sale.

     When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of those inventories         出售存貨時，其賬面值於有關收入確認期內確
     is recognised as an expense in the period in which the related              認為開支。任何存貨金額撇減至可變現淨值及
     revenue is recognised. The amount of any write-down of inventories          存貨之所有虧損均於撇減或虧損之發生期內確
     to net realisable value and all losses of inventories are recognised as     認為開支。倘存貨之撇減出現任何撥回，則於
     an expense in the period the write-down or loss occurs. The amount          撥回出現期內將費用作減額確認。
     of any reversal of any write-down of inventories is recognised as a
     reduction in the amount of inventories recognised as an expense in
     the period in which the reversal occurs.

(s)    Provisions and contingent liabilities                                (s)   撥備及或然負債
Provisions are recognised when the Group has a legal or constructive        倘若本集團須就已發生之事件承擔法律或推定

and a reliable estimate can be made. Where the time value of                幣時間價值重大，則按預計履行責任所需開支
money is material, provisions are stated at the present value of the        之現值計提撥備。
expenditure expected to settle the obligation.

Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be       倘若經濟利益外流之可能性較低，或是無法對
required, or the amount cannot be estimated reliably, the obligation        有關金額作出可靠之估計，便會將該責任披露
is disclosed as a contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow   為或然負債，但假如經濟利益流出之可能性渺
of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations, whose existence       茫則除外。須視乎一宗或多宗未來事件是否發
will only be confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one           生才能確定存在與否之潛在責任，亦會披露為
or more future events are also disclosed as contingent liabilities          或然負債，但假如這類經濟利益之流出之可能
unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is remote.           性渺茫則除外。

(w)    Borrowing costs                                                     (w)   借貸成本
Borrowing costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition,         倘一項資產需較長時間才可準備就緒用作預定
construction or production of an asset which necessarily takes a           用途或出售狀態，則直接歸屬於收購、興建或
substantial period of time to get ready for its intended use or sale are   生產該項資產之借貸成本將被資本化為該項資
capitalised as part of the cost of that asset. Other borrowing costs       產之成本之一部份。其他借貸成本在產生當期
are expensed in the period in which they are incurred.                     列作支出。

The capitalisation of borrowing costs as part of the cost of a             當資產開支及借貸成本已經產生，且為使資產
qualifying asset commences when expenditure for the asset is               可用作擬定用途或可出售狀態所必要之活動已
being incurred, borrowing costs are being incurred and activities          經開始，借貸成本即資本化為該合資格資產之
that are necessary to prepare the asset for its intended use or sale       成本之一部份。倘為使合資格資產可用作擬定
are in progress. Capitalisation of borrowing costs is suspended or         用途或可出售狀態所必需之大部份活動中止或
ceases when substantially all the activities necessary to prepare          完成，借貸成本之資本化則隨之中止或停止。
the qualifying asset for its intended use or sale are interrupted or
complete.

     of the individual segments, bank loans, lease liabilities and other          接管理之租賃負債及其他負債，惟僱員退休福
     liabilities managed directly by the segments with the exception of           利負債、遞延稅項負債、應付現期稅項及未分
     employee retirement benefit liabilities, deferred tax liabilities, current   配之總公司及企業負債除外。
     tax payable and unallocated head office and corporate liabilities.

     The measure used for reporting segment profit is “profit from                用於報告分部溢利之表示方法為「經營溢利」。
     operations”. To arrive at “profit from operations”, the Group’s profit       為了得出「經營溢利」，本集團之溢利就並無明
     is further adjusted for items not specifically attributed to individual      確 歸 於 個 別 分 部 之 項 目（如 融 資 成 本、所 佔 合
     segments, such as finance costs, share of losses of joint venture,           營公司虧損、與承擔 Vitasoy Australia Products
     integration expenses in relation to the assumption of full ownership         Pty. Ltd.（「VAP」）全部擁有權相關的整合開支以
     of Vitasoy Australia Products Pty. Ltd. (“VAP”) and unallocated              及未分配之總公司及企業費用）作出進一步調整。
     head office and corporate expenses. Income tax is not allocated to           所得稅並無列入報告分部。分部間銷售乃按成
     reporting segments. Inter-segment sales are priced at cost plus a            本加邊際利潤定價。
     profit margin.

(d)    The fair value of investment properties (continued)                (d)   投資物業之公允值（續）
The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3 valuation          公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號「公
under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS            允值計量 」所 界 定 之 三 級 公 允 值 架 構 分 類 為 第
13, Fair value measurement . The level into which a fair value            三級估值。將某公允值計量分類之等級乃經參
measurement is classified is determined with reference to                 考如下估值方法所用數據之可觀察性及重要性
observability and significance of the inputs used in the valuation        後釐定：
technique as follows:

–      Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only Level 1         －     第一級估值：僅使用第一級輸入數據（即
       inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for               於計量日同類資產或負債於活躍市場之
       identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date;                 未經調整報價）計量之公允值；

–      Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs       －     第二級估值：使用第二級輸入數據（即未
       i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not using         能達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未
       significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs are                 使用重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允
       inputs for which market data are not available; and                      值。不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數 據 為 無 市 場 數 據 之
                                                                                輸入數據；及

–      Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant          －     第 三 級 估 值：使 用 重 大 不 可 觀 察 輸 入 數
       unobservable inputs.                                                     據計量之公允值。

The fair value of investment properties is determined by using the         投資物業之公允值乃參考可比較物業之市價使
direct comparison approach with reference to the market price of           用直接比較法釐定，並就參考交易之樓宇質素
comparable properties and adjusted for building quality and timing         及時間作出調整。公允值計量之重大不可觀察
of the reference transaction. The significant unobservable input           輸入數據乃個別物業調整率，介乎 0.94 至 1.05
in the fair value measurement is the property-specific adjusting          （二 零 二 三 年：0.93 至 1.03）。公 允 值 計 量 與 個
rate, which ranged from 0.94 to 1.05 (2023: 0.93 to 1.03). The fair       別物業調整率之間成正向關係。
value measurement is positively correlated to the property-specific
adjusting rate.

       Group. The employees are entitled to 100% of the employer’s                  開 處 理。在 參 與 計 劃 滿 十 年 後，僱 員 可
       contributions after ten completed membership years, or at an                 享 有 全 部 僱 主 供 款，而 參 與 計 劃 達 五 至

       corporate trustee. Under the MPF scheme, the Group and the                   根 據 強 制 性 公 積 金 計 劃，本 集 團 及 僱 員

(d)    Long service payments                                              (d)   長期服務金
Hong Kong employees that have been employed continuously for              根據香港《僱傭條例》，在若干情況下，香港僱
at least five years are entitled to long service payments (“LSP”) in      員持續服務至少五年可享有長期服務金（「長期
accordance with the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance under                  服務金」）。該等情況包括僱員因嚴重不當行為
certain circumstances. These circumstances include where an               或 裁 員 以 外 之 原 因 遭 到 解 僱、僱 員 於 65 歲 或
employee is dismissed for reasons other than serious misconduct or        以上退休或僱傭合約為固定期限且屆滿後不予
redundancy, that employee resigns at the age of 65 or above, or the       續約。釐定長期服務金應付款項乃參考僱員最
employment contract is of fixed term and expires without renewal.         後薪金（上限為 22,500 元）及服務年期，並扣減
The amount of LSP payable is determined with reference to the             本集團強積金計劃（見附註 22(a)(i)）或界定供款
employee’s final salary (capped at $22,500) and the years of service,     計 劃（見 附 註 22(a)(i)）或 界 定 福 利 計 劃（見 附 註
reduced by the amount of any accrued benefits derived from the            22(c)）歸屬於本集團所作供款之任何應計權益，
Group’s contribution to the MPF scheme (see note 22(a)(i)), or the        每 名 僱 員 之 總 上 限 為 390,000 元。本 集 團 現 時
DC Plan (see note 22(a)(i)) or the DB Plan (see note 22(c)), with an      並無任何獨立資金安排以履行其長期服務金義務。
overall cap of $390,000 per employee. Currently, the Group does
not have any separate funding arrangement in place to meet its LSP
obligation.

In June 2022, the Hong Kong SAR Government (the “Government”)             於二零二二年六月，香港特別行政區政府（「政
gazetted the Hong Kong Employment and Retirement Scheme                   府」）刊憲《2022 年僱傭及退休計劃法例（抵銷安
Legislation (Offsetting Arrangement) (Amendment) Ordinance 2022           排）
                                                                           （修訂）條例》  （「該修訂條例」），其將自二零
(the “Amendment Ordinance”), which will come into effect from 1st         二五年五月一日起生效（「過渡日期」）。該修訂
May 2025 (the “Transition Date”). The Amendment Ordinance will            條例將最終廢除僱主可利用其向強積金計劃作
eventually abolish the statutory right of an employer to reduce its       出之強制性供款減少其應付僱員之長期服務金
LSP payable to a Hong Kong employee by drawing on its mandatory           之法定權利。
contributions to the MPF scheme.

Among other things, once the abolition of the offsetting mechanism        其中，一旦取消對沖機制生效，僱主自過渡日
takes effect, an employer can no longer use any of the accrued            期 起 概 不 得 使 用 其 強 制 性 強 積 金 供 款（無 論 於
benefits derived from its mandatory MPF contributions (irrespective       過渡日期之前、當日或之後作出之供款）所產生
of the contributions made before, on or after the Transition Date)        之任何累計權益減少有關僱員服務年期之長期
to reduce the LSP in respect of an employee’s service from the            服務金。然而，倘僱員於過渡日期前已開始受僱，
Transition Date. However, where an employee’s employment                  則僱主可繼續使用上述累計權益減少截至該日
commenced before the Transition Date, the employer can continue           期前就僱員服務年期之長期服務金；另外，就
to use the above accrued benefits to reduce the LSP in respect of         過渡日期前服務年期之長期服務金將按僱員緊
the employee’s service up to that date; in addition, the LSP in respect   接過渡日期前之月薪及截至過渡日期之服務年
of the service before the Transition Date will be calculated based        期計算。
on the employee’s monthly salary immediately before the Transition
Date and the years of the service up to that date.

The Group has accounted for the offsetting mechanism and its              誠 如 附 註 1(q)(ii) 所 披 露，本 集 團 已 對 抵 銷 機 制
abolition as disclosed in note 1(q)(ii).                                  及其取消安排進行會計處理。

(g)    Capital management                                                  (g)   資本管理
The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to                本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團
safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, so that      能持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格
it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for       訂於與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，
other stakeholders, by pricing products and services commensurately        使其能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關
with the level of risk and by securing access to finance at a              人士提供利益。
reasonable cost.

The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital           本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder             以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄
returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings and        厚資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並
the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital position,          因應經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in
economic conditions.

Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital         本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比
structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For this       率作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本
purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bills payable, bank        集團將負債界定為應付票據、銀行貸款及租賃
loans and lease liabilities, and adjusted capital as all components of     負債之總額，經調整資本為權益之所有組成部
equity less unaccrued proposed dividends.                                  份減非累計擬派股息。

The Group’s strategy, which was unchanged from the year ended              本集團之策略與截至二零二三年三月三十一日
31st March 2023, was to maintain the debt-to-adjusted capital ratio        止年度保持不變，將負債對經調整資本比率維
below 30%. In order to maintain or adjust the ratio, the Group may         持 於 30% 以 下。為 保 持 或 調 整 有 關 比 率，本 集
adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue new             團或會調整向股東派付之股息金額、發行新股
shares, return capital to shareholders, raise new debt financing or sell   份、向股東退回資本、籌集新債項融資或出售
assets to reduce debt.                                                     資產以減低負債。

26 Financial risk management and fair values of                             26 財務風險管理及金融工具之公
   financial instruments                                                       允值

Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks arises in   本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資金、
the normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s exposures to         利率及貨幣風險。本集團面臨之有關風險及本
these risks and the financial risk management policies and practices        集團就管理該等風險所採用之財務風險管理政
used by the Group to manage these risks are described below.                策與慣例載述如下。


(a)    Credit risk                                                          (a)   信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its      信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集
contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group.         團財務虧損之風險。

The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other        本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應
receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and exposure to        收款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關
credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.                               信貸風險持續受到監控。

The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial                本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸
institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management consider         評級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信
the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                                 貸風險為低。

The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose the            本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險
Group to credit risk.                                                       之擔保。

Trade debtors and bills receivable (which are included in trade             應收賬款及應收票據（已包括於應收賬款
and other receivables)                                                      及其他應收款內）
Individual credit evaluations are performed on all customers                所有要求超過特定信貸金額之客戶均須進行個
requiring credit over a certain amount. These evaluations focus on          別信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往之到期
the customer’s past history of making payments, when due, and               還款記錄及現時還款能力，並會考慮客戶之特
current ability to pay, and take into account information specific to       定資料以及有關客戶營運所在之經濟環境。應
the customer as well as pertaining to the economic environment in           收賬款及應收票據一般於發單日起計一至三個
which the customer operates. Trade debtors and bills receivables are        月內到期。一般而言，本集團不會從客戶取得
generally due within one to three months from the date of billing.          抵押品。
Normally, the Group does not obtain collateral from customers.

The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk in                 本集團於客戶經營業務所在之行業或國家並
industries or countries in which the customers operate. Significant          無重大集中信貸風險。重大集中信貸風險主要
concentrations of credit risk primarily arise when the Group has             於 本 集 團 面 臨 個 別 客 戶 之 重 大 風 險 時 出 現。
significant exposure to individual customers. At the end of the              於 匯 報 日，本 集 團 最 大 客 戶 及 五 大 客 戶 之 欠
reporting period, 18.0% (2023: 19.0%) and 44.6% (2023: 45.0%)                款分別佔應收賬款及其他應收款總額之 18.0%
of the total trade and other receivables was due from the Group’s           （二零二三年：19.0%）及 44.6%（二零二三年：
largest customer and the five largest customers respectively.                45.0%）。

     (a)    Credit risk (continued)                                           (a)    信貸風險（續）
     Trade debtors and bills receivable (which are included in trade          應收賬款及應收票據（已包括於應收賬款

rates had been applied to re-measure those financial instruments        集團於匯報日持有令本集團面對外匯風險之該

payables and receivables within the Group which are denominated in      收款。分析不包括因換算香港以外地區附屬公
a currency other than the functional currencies of the lender or the    司及合營公司之財務報表為本集團之呈列貨幣
borrower. The analysis excludes differences that would result from      而產生之差額、來自集團實體之借貸（實質構成

currency risk of highly probable forecast transactions or committed
